They could be task inputs to the animal, but inputs to the animal can also be decoder outputs.\n  - Examples: time from an aligning signal, stimulus characteristics, our animal behavior such as:\n    - visual: contrast, position, orientation\n    - audio: frequency, amplitude\n    - behavior: running speed, pupil size\n  - Dimensions: (d_input, n_timepoints) for each trial. \n  - Should capture all relevant contextual information for the decoder\n  - If an input is a time such as onset of some stimulus, represent it as a binary time series. \n\n- **output**: Variables to be decoded/predicted from neural activity\n  - These are what we want the decoder to **predict**. They could be the animal's output behavior or properties of the stimulus.\n  - Output variables must be **categorical**, i.e. discrete rather than continuous. If a variable is not discrete, follow instructions from Decoder Task to discretize it. \n  - Examples: stimulus properties (contrast, orientation), choice (left/right), reaction time, position\n  - Can be time-varying or discrete values per trial. If at all possible, make it time-varying\n  - If an output is a time such as when a behavior occurred, represent it as a binary time series. \n\n- **subjects**: List of names of all unique subjects (mice).\n- **subject_idx**: Index into subjects for each session.\n  - Order should match order of sessions in `neural`, etc.\n\n- **brain_regions**: List of names of all brain regions recorded from.\n- **brain_region_idx**: Index into brain_regions for each neuron in each session.\n\n- **input_names**: Names of each input variable.\n  - Order should match order of `input` dimensions. \n\n- **output_names**: Names of each output variable.\n  - Order should match order of `output` dimensions.\n- **output_values**: Names of each output value for each output variable.\n  - Order should match categorical `output` values.\n\n- **metadata**: Descriptive information. \n  - `task_description`: Concise description of the task\n  - `temporal_alignment_event`: Concise description of temporal alignment event\n  - `off_start`: Signed time from alignment event to start of trial. Negative means before event, positive after. \n  - `off_end`: Signed time from alignment event to end of trial\n  - Add other relevant fields, e.g. `session_info`\n\n## Python environment\n\n- All required Python packages are already installed in the system Python environment.\n- Use `python3` to run any python code.\n- If you need to install additional libraries, use `pip install`.\n\n## Decoder Reference\n\n- Run this script with `python train_decoder.py <data_file_path>` to validate.\n- Optional arguments:\n  - `--verify-only`: Only verify data format and print summary, then exit (does not train decoder).\n  - `--plot-samples`: Plot sample trials from data and save to png files.\n  - `--cpu`: Force torch to use CPU only (use if GPU runs out of memory).\n- Pipe the output to the designated file so that the user can examine it.\n\n### Purpose\n\nThese functions will be used during the validation phase to ensure:\n1. argparse\nimport pickle\nimport re\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nSESSION_RE = re.compile(r'^[A-Za-z0-9]+_\\d{4}_\\d{2}_\\d{2}_\\d+(?:_swap[12])?$')\n\n\ndef _behavior_richness(dat):\n    score = len(dat)\n    if 'ft_trInd' in dat:\n        try:\n            score += int(np.asarray(dat['ft_trInd']).size)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n    return score\n\n\ndef load_behavior_sessions(data_dir: Path):\n    sessions = {}\n    session_source = {}\n    for p in sorted((data_dir / 'beh').glob('*.npy')):\n        obj = np.load(p, allow_pickle=True).item()\n        if not isinstance(obj, dict):\n            continue\n        for sess, dat in obj.items():\n            if isinstance(sess, str) and SESSION_RE.match(sess) and isinstance(dat, dict):\n                if sess not in sessions or _behavior_richness(dat) > _behavior_richness(sessions[sess]):\n                    sessions[sess] = dat\n                    session_source[sess] = p.name\n    return sessions, session_source\n\n\ndef load_neural_files(data_dir: Path):\n    files = {}\n    for p in sorted(data_dir.rglob('*_neural_data.npy')):\n        files[p.name.replace('_neural_data.npy', '')] = p\n    return files\n\n\ndef concat_spks(spks_list):\n    return np.concatenate([np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32) for x in spks_list], axis=0)\n\n\ndef infer_day(source_name: str):\n    name = source_name.lower()\n    if 'before_learning' in name or 'before_grating' in name:\n        return 1.0\n    if 'after_learning' in name or 'after_grating' in name:\n        return 5.0\n    m = re.search(r'test(\\d+)', name)\n    if m:\n        return float(m.group(1))\n    m = re.search(r'train(\\d+)', name)\n    if m:\n        return float(m.group(1))\n    return 0.0\n\n\ndef build_session(beh, beh_source, neural_path):\n    nobj = np.load(neural_path, allow_pickle=True).item()\n    spk = concat_spks(nobj['spks'])\n\n    ft_tr = np.asarray(beh['ft_trInd'], dtype=float)\n    ft_pos = np.asarray(beh['ft_PosCum'], dtype=float)\n    ft_move = np.asarray(beh['ft_move'], dtype=float)\n    ft_wall = np.asarray(beh['ft_WallID'])\n    n_frames = min(spk.shape[1], len(ft_tr), len(ft_pos), len(ft_move), len(ft_wall))\n    spk = spk[:, :n_frames]\n    ft_tr = ft_tr[:n_frames]\n    ft_pos = ft_pos[:n_frames]\n    ft_move = ft_move[:n_frames]\n    ft_wall = ft_wall[:n_frames]\n\n    valid_frame = np.isfinite(ft_tr)\n    ft_tr_int = np.full(n_frames, -1, dtype=int)\n    ft_tr_int[valid_frame] = ft_tr[valid_frame].astype(int)\n\n    wall = np.asarray(beh['WallName'])\n    is_rew = np.asarray(beh['isRew']).astype(np.int64)\n    tstart = np.asarray(beh['Trial_start_time'], dtype=float)\n    tend = np.asarray(beh['Trial_end_time'], dtype=float)\n    sound = np.asarray(beh['SoundTime'], dtype=float)\n    ntrials = int(np.asarray(beh['ntrials']).item())\n    corridor_len_arr = np.asarray(beh.get('Corridor_Length', 4.0))\n    corridor_len = float(corridor_len_arr.item()) if corridor_len_arr.shape == () else 4.0\n    day = infer_day(beh_source)\n\n    trial_dur_sec = (tend - tstart) * 86400.0\n    frame_periods = []\n    for tr in range(min(ntrials, len(trial_dur_sec))):\n        nfr = np.sum(ft_tr_int == tr)\n        if nfr > 1 and np.isfinite(trial_dur_sec[tr]) and trial_dur_sec[tr] > 0:\n            frame_periods.append(trial_dur_sec[tr] / nfr)\n    dt_frame = float(np.nanmedian(frame_periods)) if frame_periods else 0.1\n\n    valid_idx = np.flatnonzero(ft_tr_int >= 0)\n    valid_tr = ft_tr_int[valid_idx]\n    uniq_tr, starts = np.unique(valid_tr, return_index=True)\n    trial_frame_idx = {int(tr): valid_idx[starts[i]:starts[i+1]] if i+1 < len(starts) else valid_idx[starts[i]:] for i, tr in enumerate(uniq_tr)}\n\n    lick_series = np.zeros(n_frames, dtype=np.int64)\n    lick_tr = np.asarray(beh.get('LickTrind', []))\n    lick_time = np.asarray(beh.get('LickTime', []), dtype=float)\n    if lick_tr.size and lick_time.size:\n        lick_tr_i = lick_tr.astype(int)\n        for tr in np.unique(lick_tr_i):\n            idx = trial_frame_idx.get(int(tr))\n            if idx is None or idx.size == 0 or tr >= len(tstart):\n                continue\n            rel = (lick_time[lick_tr_i == tr] - tstart[int(tr)]) * 86400.0\n            rel = rel[np.isfinite(rel)]\n            if rel.size == 0:\n                continue\n            bins = np.clip(np.floor(rel / dt_frame).astype(int), 0, idx.size - 1)\n            lick_series[idx[np.unique(bins)]] = 1\n\n    neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials = [], [], []\n    all_speeds = []\n    cache = []\n    for tr, idx in trial_frame_idx.items():\n        if idx.size < 2:\n            continue\n        if tr >= len(wall) or tr >= len(is_rew) or tr >= len(sound) or tr >= len(tstart):\n            continue\n        tr_spk = spk[:, idx].astype(np.float32, copy=False)\n        pos = np.mod(ft_pos[idx], corridor_len).astype(np.float32)\n        time_since = np.arange(idx.size, dtype=np.float32) * dt_frame\n        sound_sec = float((sound[tr] - tstart[tr]) * 86400.0) if np.isfinite(sound[tr]) and np.isfinite(tstart[tr]) else np.nan\n        time_to_sound = (sound_sec - time_since).astype(np.float32) if np.isfinite(sound_sec) else np.full(idx.size, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n        day_arr = np.full(idx.size, day, dtype=np.float32)\n        reward_arr = np.full(idx.size, int(is_rew[tr]), dtype=np.float32)\n        inp = np.vstack([time_to_sound, day_arr, time_since, reward_arr]).astype(np.float32)\n\n        pos_bin = np.clip((pos / max(corridor_len, 1e-6) * 4).astype(int), 0, 3)\n        speed = np.diff(pos, prepend=pos[0]) / max(dt_frame, 1e-6)\n        lick = lick_series[idx]\n        cache.append((tr_spk, inp, str(wall[tr]), lick.astype(np.int64), pos_bin.astype(np.int64), speed.astype(np.float32)))\n        all_speeds.append(speed)\n\n    if not cache:\n        return [], [], []\n\n    speed_all = np.concatenate(all_speeds)\n    qs = np.quantile(speed_all, [0.25, 0.5, 0.75]) if speed_all.size else np.array([0, 0, 0], dtype=np.float32)\n    for tr_spk, inp, wall_name, lick, pos_bin, speed in cache:\n        speed_bin = np.digitize(speed, qs, right=False).astype(np.int64)\n        neural_trials.append(tr_spk)\n        input_trials.append(inp)\n        output_trials.append((wall_name, np.vstack([\n            np.full(tr_spk.shape[1], -1, dtype=np.int64),\n            lick,\n            pos_bin,\n            speed_bin,\n        ])))\n    return neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials\n\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    ap.add_argument('outpicklefile')\n    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=False)\n    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', default=False)\n    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true', default=False)\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n    if not args.full and not args.sample:\n        args.full = True\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    data_dir = Path('data')\n    beh_sessions, beh_source = load_behavior_sessions(data_dir)\n    neural_files = load_neural_files(data_dir)\n    common = sorted(set(beh_sessions) & set(neural_files))\n    if args.sample:\n        def sample_score(sess):\n            d = beh_sessions[sess]\n            lick_n = len(np.asarray(d.get('LickTime', [])))\n            rew = np.asarray(d.get('isRew', []))\n            rew_frac = float(np.mean(rew.astype(float))) if rew.size else 0.0\n            return (lick_n, rew_frac)\n        common = sorted(common, key=sample_score, reverse=True)[:2]\n    print(f'Loaded {len(beh_sessions)} behavior sessions, {len(neural_files)} neural sessions, {len(common)} common sessions')\n\n    wall_names = sorted({str(w) for s in common for w in np.asarray(beh_sessions[s]['WallName'])})\n    wall_to_id = {w: i for i, w in enumerate(wall_names)}\n    subjects = sorted({s.split('_')[0] for s in common})\n    subj_to_id = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n\n    data = {\n        'neural': [],\n        'input': [],\n        'output': [],\n        'subjects': subjects,\n        'subject_idx': [],\n        'brain_regions': ['unknown'],\n        'brain_region_idx': [],\n        'input_names': ['time_to_sound_cue', 'day_of_training', 'time_since_trial_start', 'reward_availability'],\n        'output_names': ['visual_stimulus_category', 'licking', 'corridor_position_bin', 'running_speed_bin'],\n        'output_values': [wall_names, ['no_lick', 'lick'], ['bin1', 'bin2', 'bin3', 'bin4'], ['q1', 'q2', 'q3', 'q4']],\n        'metadata': {\n            'task_description': 'Visual corridor task with rewarded and unrewarded corridors; decode stimulus, licking, position, and running speed from deconvolved neural activity.',\n            'time_bin_size': None,\n            'temporal_alignment_event': 'corridor entry / trial start',\n            'off_start': 0.0,\n            'off_end': None,\n        },\n    }\n\n    kept = 0\n    for sess in common:\n        neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials = build_session(beh_sessions[sess], beh_source[sess], neural_files[sess])\n        if len(neural_trials) < 2:\n            continue\n        sess_out = []\n        for wall_name, out in output_trials:\n            out = out.copy()\n            out[0, :] = wall_to_id[wall_name]\n            sess_out.append(out)\n        data['neural'].append(neural_trials)\n        data['input'].append(input_trials)\n        data['output'].append(sess_out)\n        data['subject_idx'].append(subj_to_id[sess.split('_')[0]])\n        data['brain_region_idx'].append(np.zeros(neural_trials[0].shape[0], dtype=np.int64))\n        kept += 1\n        print('kept', sess, The VRn and VRg pretraining groups of mice (Fig. 5) were trained to acquire water from the spout on t\\r\\nhe last 2 days of unsupervised pretraining with no stimuli presented, after the virtual reality session ended to avoid the associative learning between stimuli\\r\\nand rewards. For the group without pretraining (Fig. 5), learning to get reward from the spout was similarly carried out after the running training session on t\\r\\nhe last 2 days of running training.\\r\\n\\r\\nFor the behaviour-only experiment, all animals started training in the virtual reality\\u2009+\\u2009reward training session on a Monday and continued training for exactly\\r\\n5 days. The first day of training consisted of passive reward training, during which the reward was always delivered to the mouse in the rewarded corridor at th\\r\\ne beginning of the reward zone. The next 4 days consisted of active reward training, during which the mice were required to lick in the reward zone to trigger t\\r\\nhe reward. This ensured a consistent training schedule during the critical learning period. The beginning of the reward zone was randomly chosen per trial from\\r\\na uniform distribution between 2\\u2009m and 3\\u2009m, and continued until the end of the corridor. This meant that the earliest position in the rewarded corridor in which\\r\\n the mouse can receive the reward was a range of 2\\u20133\\u2009m.\\r\\n\\r\\nFor imaging mice, the sound cue was presented in all trial types, for task and unsupervised mice, and the time of the sound cue was randomly chosen per trial fr\\r\\nom a uniform distribution between positions 0.5\\u2009m and 3.5\\u2009m. For task mice, the sound cue indicated the beginning of the reward zone in the rewarded corridor. T\\r\\nhe reward was delivered if a lick was detected after the sound cue in the rewarded corridor. As mice kept licking (anticipatory licking) as soon as they entered\\r\\n the reward corridors and learned to lick right after the sound cue, the reward delivery locations were also approximately uniformly distributed between 0.5\\u2009m a\\r\\nnd 3.5\\u2009m (Fig. 1c). In some mice, the reward was delivered passively with a delay after the sound cue, but these mice still showed anticipatory licking before t\\r\\nhe sound cue. To rule out whether the licking was due to detecting signals related to the reward delivery, such as water coming out the spout or sound produced\\r\\nby solenoid valve, we considered a lick response if the mouse licked at least once inside the corridor but before the sound cue. Although the rewards were absen\\r\\nt, the sound cue was still presented in the unsupervised training experiment for consistency.\\r\\n\\r\\nProcessing of calcium imaging data\\r\\n\\r\\nCalcium imaging data were processed using Suite2p32, available on GitHub (www.github.com/MouseLand/suite2p). Suite2p performs motion correction, region of inter\\r\\nest detection, cell classification, neuropil correction and spike deconvolution as previously described64. For non-negative deconvolution, we used a timescale o\\r\\nf decay of 0.75\\u2009s (ref. 65). off-by-one mismatch. More importantly, the first 10 `ft_trInd` entries are NaN and `ft_move` is zero there, but that alone shouldn't drop the whole session. The likely reason it was excluded is that our `build_session` loop iterates over `trial_frame_idx.items()` built from valid non-NaN frames, so if a trial has only one valid frame or if all trial indices are shifted by the frame mismatch, it might still keep many trials. Yet the session was dropped, implying `build_session` returned fewer than 2 valid trials.
